local mm=string local d=math local fe=table local dv=bit32 local xh=mm.char local iu=mm.byte local vz=mm.sub local fp=mm.upper local hz=mm.lower local ck=mm.find local to=mm.len local qy=d.floor local rz=d.sqrt local eg=fe.insert local yk=fe.remove local qx=fe.concat local nr=dv.bxor local wd=dv.band local ix=dv.bor local q=dv.rshift local eq=dv.lshift local se=dv.rrotate local rm=dv.bnot local kp=dv.lrotate local hl=rawset local qc=rawget local zc=tostring local um=tonumber local a=(function(ul,un,uo,up,uq,ur,us,ut,uu,uv,uw)local b=[[!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!G!`HPo!8U#dV$^a@RNfj0+;_O2!F>D[WJL'BicAY3"e/<n*SXr\q-76?K&!!!ts!mE]l9Q,5g%bv14h.(=I)Cu:MkTZ!p!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!!]]local function uz(c)local e=uv local function f(g)local h=e(b,e(c,g)+1)or 33 local i=h-33 return i>0 and i-1 or 0 end local j=f(1)+f(2)*85 local k=f(3)local l={}for m=4,#c-4,5 do local n=0 for o=0,4 do n=n*85+f(m+o)end l[#l+1]=un(ut(n,24),0xFF);l[#l+1]=un(ut(n,16),0xFF)l[#l+1]=un(ut(n,8),0xFF);l[#l+1]=un(n,0xFF)end for p=1,k do l[#l]=nil end return l end local function ux(r,s,t)local u=r[s]or 0 return un(ul(u,un(t*131+s,0xFF)),0xFF)end local function uy(v,w)local x=uz(v)local y=x[1]local z=2 local aa=ux(x,z,y);z=z+1 local ab=un(ul(ux(x,z,y),aa),0xFF);z=z+1 local ac={}for ad=1,ab do ac[ad]=uo(un(ul(ux(x,z,y),aa),0xFF));z=z+1 end local ae=up(ac)local af={}for ad=1,6 do af[ad]=un(ul(ux(x,z,y),aa),0xFF);z=z+1 end local ag=af[5]local ah=af[1]local ai=af[2]local aj=af[6]local ak=af[3]local al=af[4]local am=ai+aj*256 local an={}for ad=1,ak do an[ad]=un(ul(ux(x,z,y),aa),0xFF);z=z+1 end local function ao()local ap=ag local aq={}for ad=1,ak do aq[ad]=uo(un(ul(an[ad],ap),0xFF))ap=un(ap*ah+ad,0xFF)end return up(aq)end if al==1 then local ar=function(as)local at=0 if uq(as)=="table"then for au,av in ipairs(as)do at=(at+av)%65537 end end if(at+w)%65537~=am then return nil end return uu[ao()]end ur(uu,ae,ar)elseif al==3 then local aw=function(as)local at=0 if uq(as)=="table"then for au,av in ipairs(as)do at=(at+av)%65537 end end if(at+w)%65537~=am then return nil end local ax=ao()local ay=loadstring(ax)if not ay then return nil end return ay()end ur(uu,ae,aw)else local az=nil local fn=function(as)if az~=nil then return az end local at=0 if uq(as)=="table"then for au,av in ipairs(as)do at=(at+av)%65537 end end if(at+w)%65537~=am then return nil end az=ao()return az end ur(uu,ae,fn)end local ba=w for ad=1,#ae do ba=(un(ba*31,0xFFFF)+uv(ae,ad)+ad)%65537 end return ba end return function(bb)local bc=0 for ad=1,#bb do bc=uy(bb[ad],bc)end end end)(bit32.bxor,bit32.band,string.char,table.concat,type,rawset,rawget,bit32.rshift,(getfenv and getfenv(1))or _ENV or
